  "description": "# Initiative — workflow governance, single-sourced and installable anywhere\n\n**Goal:** For anyone who wants to work the way I do — me included, on a new machine — we want our workflow governance to be adoptable on its own, separate from the Shaper methodology, so adopting Shaper never forces a tracker and the patterns travel to any machine unchanged.\n\n## Key results\n\n**KR1 (commit)** — Governance installs and loads on a clean machine with zero per-machine config. *(bet)*\n- baseline: loads only via absolute-path `@`-imports in `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`; no install path exists.\n- target: on a clean profile, installing the pack makes a new session show the GOVERNANCE index (injected by the SessionStart hook), with 0 rule `@`-imports left in `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`.\n- measured over: by cycle close.\n- how we'll know: install on a clean Claude profile; inspect SessionStart output; `grep -n \"@\" ~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`.\n\n*Layer 1: operability · Layer 2: golden-path test*\n\n**KR2 (commit)** — Shaper carries no tracker dependency; the Linear artefacts live only in the new pack. *(bet)*\n- baseline: `linear-workflow.md` + `skills/initiative-shape` live in Shaper; its skills/AGENTS reference Linear.\n- target: those artefacts no longer exist under `agent-skills-shaper`; `grep -rni \"linear|initiative-shape\"` across the Shaper pack returns nothing.\n- measured over: by cycle close.\n- how we'll know: `ls` + `grep -rni \"linear\\|initiative-shape\" /Users/anton/src/agent-skills-shaper/`.\n\n*Layer 1: correctness · Layer 2: artefact-absent + grep-clean*\n\n**KR3 (commit)** — Each wired repo points once at the single source and restates nothing. *(brake)*\n- baseline: 3 AGENTS.md restate the Linear workflow; 1 carries the \"four-field\" drift; 2 carry stale `mcp__linear-server__` names.\n- target: each in-scope AGENTS.md has one path-free pack pointer; `grep -rn \"## Linear workflow\" / \"four-field\" / \"mcp__linear-server__\"` across them = 0.\n- measured over: by cycle close.\n- how we'll know: the three greps across agent-skills-shaper, agent-skills, nestl, stock-review all return 0.\n\n*Layer 1: discipline · Layer 2: fitness function (grep-clean)*\n\n**KR4 (commit)** — A second install at a pinned version reproduces the same governance, and the iterate loop is documented. *(foundation)*\n- baseline: no marketplace entry, no version, no install/iterate docs.\n- target: versioned plugin manifest + marketplace entry + README documenting install and the edit→bump→push→update loop; the pinned version reproduces the same index on a second profile.\n- measured over: by cycle close.\n- how we'll know: inspect manifest (version) + README (install + iterate sections); install pinned version on a second profile and diff the injected index.\n\n*Layer 1: maintenance · Layer 2: artefact-exists + reproduce-on-second-install*\n\n*Dimensions: operability / correctness / discipline / maintenance — all distinct (rubric rule 5b). Roles: 2 bet + 1 brake + 1 foundation.*\n\n## Affected repos\n\nNEW `agent-skills-linear-workflow` (plugin handle `linear-workflow`); `agent-skills-shaper`; `agent-skills`; `nestl`; `stock-review`; + global `~/.claude` config (not a repo). **EXCLUDED:** `adyen-onboarding` (Tolaria vault, not a code project).\n\n## Appetite\n\n~9 issues (Medium — one full cycle slot).\n\n## Kill condition\n\nThe SessionStart-hook loading proves unreliable in practice (governance fails to inject, or principle-grounding degrades). Fallback: ship the pack without the hook and load `GOVERNANCE.md` via one documented per-machine import line.\n\n## Project type\n\n1 (methodology).\n\n---\n\n*Rubric note (Step 6.5):* this is a one-time packaging/distribution refactor, so its KRs are structural (binary / fitness-function / state-change — all permitted KR forms) rather than the type-1 default invocation-rate + decision-quality mix, which assumes a methodology-adoption initiative. Deviation recorded deliberately; structural KRs are the right fit here."
}

Highest correctness stakes in the initiative.\n\n## Scope\n\nAuthor `rules/GOVERNANCE.md` — the short always-on index the SessionStart hook (issue 4) injects every session.\n\n**Hybrid structure:**\n\n* *Inline* the always-on short rules: the Linear lifecycle mechanics (six-field initiative check, 5-step completion gate, 3 initiatives + 1 ops slot, \"Done = KRs observed not issues closed\") **and the comment standard** (moved here from the global `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` — issue 6 removes it there).\n* *Point* (prose, **not** `@`-import) to the bulky reference rules with a \"read when\" trigger: `linear-workflow.md` (full rubric, in-pack) and — *\"if the Shaper pack is installed\"* — `PRODUCT_RULES.md` / `eng-principles-*.md`.\n\n**Constraints:**\n\n* Target \\~50 lines. The index is a pointer map, not a copy — restate nothing that lives in `linear-workflow.md` beyond the always-on mechanics.\n* The inlined mechanics must **agree** with `linear-workflow.md` (six fields, not four; the 5-step completion gate; Done = KRs observed).\n* Voice matches the existing rule files — direct, no filler.\n\n## Verification — run by a subagent, not self-certified\n\n**Subagent model:** Sonnet 4.6 (semantic check: \"index matches the canonical rubric\").\n**Prompt for the verification subagent:**\n\n> You are verifying an always-on governance index against its canonical source, read-only. Do **not** edit anything to make a check pass. Report **PASS/FAIL per check with raw evidence** (file excerpts, line counts) — never \"looks correct\".\n>\n> 1. `agent-skills-linear-workflow/rules/GOVERNANCE.md` exists and is ≤ \\~60 lines. (Paste `wc -l`.)\n> 2. Its Linear mechanics **agree** with `rules/linear-workflow.md`: six-field initiative check (not four), the 5-step completion gate, 3 initiatives + 1 ops slot, and \"Done = KRs observed, not issues closed\". Quote the matching lines from **both** files side by side. Flag any contradiction.\n> 3. The comment standard is present inline. (Quote it.)\n> 4. References to the bulky rules are **prose pointers, not** `@`**-imports**: `grep -n \"@\" GOVERNANCE.md` shows no `@`-import lines. Shaper-principle pointers are conditional (\"if … installed\"). (Paste output.)\n> 5. Do **not** edit anything to make a check pass. (Quote both.)\n> 4. Confirm governance still reaches a session by another path: a new session's SessionStart output shows the GOVERNANCE index (the hook, not the removed imports). (Paste the index excerpt.)\n> On any FAIL: report the gap and return the issue to In Progress. Check 4 is the safety interlock — if it fails, governance was lost; restore the imports until the hook works."
